4. Active set quadratic solver: At each major step, choose active coefficients K (top maybe 5% or within 0.001 of max). Actually output length active. We can solve using conjugate gradient on normal equations: (B^T B + λ I)d = -B^T r. B and B^T can be applied via FFT? B d = conv(a,d) restricted to active rows. B^T y = cross convolution of y with a as in gradient. We can use scipy.sparse? But K size ~1000, n=7000. Simpler: d = -α grad_active with projection; gradient already least squares with λ=1. Could use adaptive λ.

### Internal objective and FFT helpers

```python
def fft_conv_same(a,b):
    M=len(a)+len(b)-1
    fft_len=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, fft_len)
    B=np.fft.rfft(b, fft_len)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*B, fft_len)[:M]
```
For self conv `fft_conv(a,a)`.

### Simplex projection
Implement `project_simplex(v)`: Projection of v onto {x>=0, sum=1}. Standard:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    n=v.size
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)-1
    ind=np.arange(n)+1
    cond=u - cssv/ind >0
    rho=ind[cond].max()
    theta=cssv[cond].max()/rho
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
But project onto sum=1; if v sum differs. This projects. Works for sum=1 target. Since objective scale invariant, sum1.

### Active gradient weights and step

```
def weighted_grad(a, w):
    # grad_j = 2 sum_k w_k a_{k-j}
    ar=a[::-1]
    M=len(ar)+len(w)-1
    L=power2(M)
    AR=np.fft.rfft(ar,L)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(AR*W,L)[:M]
    return 2.0*conv[len(a)-1:2*len(a)-1]
```
Check length: ar length n, w length 2n-1 -> M=3n-2. conv[n-1:2n-2] length n. Good.

Weights for LSE active:
```
def lse_weights_and_value(c, beta, c_ref=None):
    # Avoid overflow: z=beta*c, shift max
    zmax=c.max()
    z=beta*(c-zmax)
    exp_z=np.exp(z); p=exp_z/exp_z.sum()
    L = zmax + logsumexp/zeta? = zmax + (log(exp_sum))/beta
    return L,p
```

### Spectrum shaping / error reduction

Given a sum1. Compute c via FFT. Choose target threshold M = c_mean + gamma*(cmax-cmean)? Or simply clip above max*0.95. We want reduce peaks. A good method: set desired c_target = min(c, M_linear) with M_linear = alpha*cmax + (1-alpha)*cmean, maybe M = target current max*stag. Then delta = c_target - c (negative at peaks). We want ΔQ = FFT(delta) (periodic) but linear convolution zero padding length M_fft >=2n-1. U = rfft(a, M_fft). Update U += λ * ΔU / (2U) with zero for small U. Then a_new=irfft(U). We need preserve DC: set U[0] = sum(a) =1 (or adjust). Clip/normalize. Line search on λ. This is not exact Newton for linear conv because using circular FFT length 2n-1; zero padding avoids wrap, U^2 inverse equals full conv up to length. Changing U modifies c with quadratic terms; small λ okay. We'll implement robust.

```
def spectrum_shaping(a, gamma=0.8, lam=0.5):
  c=conv(a,a)
  Mval = c.max()
  Mavg = c.mean()
  target = Mavg + (1-gamma)*(Mval-Mavg)? gamma closer1 reduces more. If gamma=0.8 target=mean+0.2(range) reduces top.
  excess = np.maximum(c-target, 0)
  delta = -excess
  # preserve total sum: delta -= delta.mean()/(2n-1)? Because sum c fixed; if we subtract excess, total low. Add constant to keep sum: delta += excess.sum()/len(c)
  # Now ΔQ idft? Actually Q=DFT(c) (linear zero-padded circular length L>=2n-1). We have time-domain delta, need freq delta_f=fft(delta)
  L=fft_len
  U=rfft(a,L)
  with errstate: dU = fft(delta)/(2U)
  dU[abs(U)<eps]=0
  a_try=ifft(U+lam*dU).real[:n]
  clip, normalize
```
But ΔQ must be shape of U^2? Since Q=U^2 (circular length L). If we adjust Q by ΔQ (idft = FFT(delta)), then new U approx sqrt(Q+ΔQ) ≈ U + ΔQ/(2U). Yes. Enforce U[0]=S (real) maybe: `U[0]=1`. Also ensure symmetry? rfft handles.
Line search lam candidates [0.2,0.1,0.5,1]? The update can be large; use small.

### Parametric continuous details

Let's write functions:
```
def build_basis_odd(n, K):
    x=np.arange(n, dtype=float)/(n-1)
    basis=np.empty((K,n))
    for k in range(K):
      basis[k]=np.cos((2*k+1)*np.pi*x)
    return basis
```
For general sine/cos:
```

   x=...

   for idx,m in enumerate(freqs): basis[2idx]=np.cos(2π m x); basis[2idx+1]=np.sin(...)

Parametric score wrapper top-level:
```
def score_poly_coeffs(params, basis, scale=1.0, dtype=np.float64):
   # params may include param0 as offset? We'll use just coefficients for basis; a=1+g
   if params.size==0: a=np.ones(basis.shape[1])
   else: g=basis.dot(params)
   minf=1.0+float(g.min())
   if minf < 0:
      # scale to ensure min 1e-6
      factor = 0.999 / max(1.0, -minf)
      g *= factor
   a = 1.0 + g
   # maybe clip tiny negatives
   a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
   S=a.sum()
   if S<=0: return inf, None
   a=a/S
   return score_internal(a), a
```
But if g has nonzero mean, normalization changes shape scaling? a=1+g, sum may not be 0 mean; after normalize, the relative scale of g effectively divided by mean. Fine. def optimize_active_gradient(a0, budget_s=600, beta_start=500, beta_end=5000, seed=1, min_beta=100, eps=1e-15):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a=np.maximum(a0,0); S=a.sum(); if S<... a=ones/n else a/S
    best_a=a.copy(); best_f=score_internal(a)
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    beta=beta_start
    while time.monotonic()<t_start+budget_s-5:
       if it % 50 ==0 and no_improve>0:
          # adapt beta
       c=conv_self(a); F=c.max()
       # compute weights maybe if beta schedule based on it
       # Instead of fixed beta, each phase:
       p = soft_weights(c, beta, top_frac=?)
       g=weighted_grad(a,p)
       norm2=dot(g,g)
       if norm2<...: break
       scale = F / (norm2 + 1e-30) # ~O(1)
       # Try steps
       accepted=False
       for mult in [0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001]:
          eta=mult*scale
          a_try=project_simplex(a-eta*g)
          Stry=a_try.sum(); if Stry<... continue
          F_try=score_internal(a_try) # c max
          if F_try < best_f*(1-1e-12):
             a=a_try; best_f=F_try; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0; accepted=True
             break
       if not accepted:
          # tiny step or reweight
          a_try=project_simplex(a-1e-6*scale*g) ...
       no_improve+=1
       it+=1
       # if no_improve > 100: perturb or increase beta
       if no_improve > 80:
          # random perturbation preserving sum
          a = a*(1+0.01*rng.standard_normal(n)); clip; normalize
          no_improve=0
          beta*=1.5 (or reset schedule)
    return best_a,best_f,seed
```

def lse_weights(c, beta):
   zmax=c.max()
   z=beta*(c-zmax)
   z-=z.max() # redundant if zero max
   ez=np.exp(z); ez/=ez.sum(); return ez
```

```

#### LSE L-BFGS

Top-level:
```
def objective_lse(x, beta):
   n=len(x); c=conv_self(x); S=x.sum(); if S<... return 1e10, zeros
   z=beta*(c-c.max()); ez=exp(z-z.max()); p=ez/sum; L=c.max()+np.log(ez.sum())/beta
   val=n*L/(S*S)
   grad_L=weighted_grad(x,p) # 2*...
   grad=n*(grad_L/(S*S) - 2*L/(S**3))
   return val, grad
```

def lp_fista(a, M, iters, beta_start, beta_end, x_init=None):
   n=len(a); L=2n-1; x init x_init or ones/n; u copy; t=1
   sum_a2=dot(a,a)
   for it:
      beta = ramp
      c=conv(a,x) # x sum maybe grows

      # B^T p (no factor 2)
      bp = cross_corr_no2(a, p)  # ∑_k p_k a_{k-j}
      grad = bp - 1.0
      L = beta * (sum maybe operator norm). Use operator norm approx: n*sum_a2? Since a sum1, sum_a2>=1/n, n*sum_a2>=1. L=beta*max(1.0, n*sum_a2)+1e-8
      step=1/L
      v=u-step*grad; x_new=max(v,0)
      FISTA momentum
   return x
```

```
def optimize_spectrum(a0, budget_s=300, n_targets=4, seed=1):
  a=normalize; best=score
  t_start...
  gamma_seq = [0.5,0.6,0.7,0.8,0.9,0.95]?

    target = c.mean() + (1-gamma)*(c.max()-c.mean()) # if gamma high near max
    # Or target = c.max()*(1 - 0.1*(1-gamma)?)

      improved=False

        a_try=update_spectrum(a,target,lam)
        sc=score(a_try)

      # update target = best_target? 
  return best_a,best
```

Function `update_spectrum`:
```
def spectrum_update(a, target, lam):
  n=len(a); L=2n-1; Lf=nextpow2(L)
  U=np.fft.rfft(a, Lf)
  c=np.fft.irfft(U*U, Lf)[:L]
  excess=np.maximum(c-target,0.0)
  delta = -excess.copy()
  # preserve total sum: add mean excess so sum delta=0?
  delta += excess.sum()/L
  # zero small U
  df=np.fft.rfft(delta, Lf)
  denom=2*U
  dU=np.zeros_like(U, complex)
  ok=np.abs(denom)>1e-12
  dU[ok]=df[ok]/denom[ok]
  U_new=U+lam*dU
  U_new[0]=a.sum() # parameter? preserve sum
  a_try=np.fft.irfft(U_new, Lf)[:n]
  a_try=np.maximum(a_try,0)
  S=a_try.sum()
  if S<... return a
  return a_try/S
```
Using FFT of delta padded to Lf; but `delta` length L zero-padded? Need create `dpad=np.zeros(Lf); dpad[:L]=delta`. Then rfft.
This update assumes Q=U^2 exact. It reduces high coefficients but may introduce ringing. Line search λ. Good.
Potential overflow if U small; ok.

Could there be imaginary residual? irfft real.

### LP alternating corrected details

If implemented, need time. Let's define:
```
def cross_no2(a,p):
    n=len(a); L=len(p); M=n+L-1; Lf=power2(M)
    ar=a[::-1]
    AR=np.fft.rfft(ar,Lf)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,Lf)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(AR*P,Lf)[:M]
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
For B^T p (x variable) is same as weighted_grad/2. Good.

Since a sum1, row sums for k=0..L-1 are sum valid a ≤1. Matrix entries nonnegative. Operator norm ≤ max(row sum + col sum?)? For nonnegative, ||B||_2 ≤ sqrt(max row sum * max col sum). Row sum ≤1. Column sum = ∑_k a_{k-j} over k length L =1 (for j within) (provided full a). So ||B||≤1. Wait for a sum1, column sum = S=1. So norm≤1. Thus gradient Lipschitz = beta*||B||²? For f(logsumexp(βBx)), gradient = B^T p; Hessian = β B^T diag(p) B; norm ≤ β * max diag? p sum1, diag≤1, ||B||²≤1. So L=beta. Previous L=beta*n*sum_a2 overestimates. But if a sum not1, B column sum=S; norm≤S; Hessian ≤ β S²; with S=1 L=β. Good. Use step=1/(beta+1e-8). Nice. But if x not normalized? x variable no sum constraint; L unaffected by x. Good.

### Let's detail helper functions

`conv_self(a)`:
```
def conv_self(a):
    n=len(a)
    M=2*n-1
    L=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:M]
    return c
```
For large n, rfft input real; A complex. `A*A` not conjugate. Good.

`score_sequence(a)`:
```
def score_sequence(a):
    n=len(a); S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-300: return np.inf
    c=conv_self(a)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0: m=0.0
    return 2.0*float(n)*m/(S*S)
```
Could have small negative due FFT; max positive.

`normalize_nonneg(a)`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<=0: return np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
    return a/S
```

`project_simplex(v)` as above. Need handle if v all equal? Works.

`cross_weights_grad(a,w)`:
```
def cross_weights_grad(a,w, factor=2.0):
    n=len(a); L=len(w); M=n+L-1; Lf=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
    ar=a[::-1]
    AR=np.fft.rfft(ar, Lf)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w, Lf)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(AR*W, Lf)[:M]
    return factor*conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
If w length not 2n-1? For LSE weights length full. For p length maybe L. Good.

`lse_weights(c, beta_scale)`:
```
def lse_weights(c, beta_scale):
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 1e-300: beta=1.0
    else: beta=float(beta_scale)/cmax
    z=beta*(c-cmax)
    # max z is 0; for stability shift
    z -= z.max() # should 0 but if cmax not included?
    ez=np.exp(z)
    s=ez.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): p=np.ones_like(c)/len(c)
    else: p=ez/s
    return p
```
But if beta huge and z max 0, exp(0)=1, okay. We can optionally rank cap.

`topk_weights(c, frac=0.01)`:
```
def topk_weights(c, frac):
    L=len(c); K=max(1, int(frac*L))
    if K>=L: return np.ones(L)/L
    idx=np.argpartition(c, L-K)[-K:]
    w=np.zeros(L)
    w[idx]=1.0/K
    return w
```

`spectrum_update(a,target,lam)`:
Need Lf for p; compute c inside or pass c to avoid recompute? We'll pass c.
```
def spectrum_update(a, c, target, lam):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1; Lf=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    U=np.fft.rfft(a, Lf)
    excess=np.maximum(c-target, 0.0)
    if excess.max() <= 0: return a
    delta = -excess.astype(np.float64)
    delta += excess.sum()/L
    dpad=np.zeros(Lf); dpad[:L]=delta
    df=np.fft.rfft(dpad)
    denom=2.0*U
    dU=np.zeros_like(U, dtype=np.complex128)
    ok=np.abs(denom) > 1e-14 * max(1.0, np.max(np.abs(U)))
    dU[ok]=df[ok]/denom[ok]
    U_new=U + lam*dU
    U_new[0]=float(np.sum(a))
    a_new=np.fft.irfft(U_new, Lf)[:n]
    a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0.0)
    S=a_new.sum()
    if S<1e-300: return a.copy()
    return a_new/S
```
But if `U` has very small values, division; okay. `ok` using U abs. What if `U_new` negative DC? set.

`optimize_spectrum(a0, budget_s, seed)`:
```
def optimize_spectrum(a0, budget_s=300, seed=1):
  a=normalize_nonneg(a0); best_a=a.copy(); best_f=score_sequence(a); c=conv_self(a)
  t0=time.monotonic(); limit=t0+budget_s
  # target list from aggressive to mild
  fractions = [0.6,0.7,0.8,0.9,0.95,0.98] # target = cmean + fraction*(cmax-cmean)? Or target = fraction*cmax? 
  # Maybe use target = max(cmean, 0.95*cmax) etc.
  for frac in [0.6,0.7,0.8,0.85,0.9,0.95]:
     if time>limit: break
     while time<limit-2:
       cmax=float(c.max()); cmean=float(c.mean())
       # target: blend mean to max; frac closer 1 less reduction
       target = cmean + frac*(cmax-cmean)
       # Ensure target < cmax - small
       if cmax <= target + 1e-15: break
       improved=False
       for lam in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.025,0.5]:
          if time>limit: break
          a_try=spectrum_update(a,c,target,lam)
          f_try=score_sequence(a_try)
          if f_try < best_f:
             a=a_try; best_f=ftry; best_a=a.copy(); c=conv_self(a); improved=True; break
       if not improved: break
  return best_a,best_f
```

### L-BFGS objective and use

Top-level:
```
def lse_objective(x, beta):
   n=len(x)
   c=conv_self(x)
   S=float(x.sum())
   if S<1e-300: return 1e10, np.zeros(n)
   z=beta*(c-c.max())
   # shift
   zmax=z.max()
   ez=np.exp(z-zmax)
   s=ez.sum()
   p=ez/s
   L=float(c.max()) + (float(zmax)+np.log(s))/beta # Wait c.max + (zmax + log s)/beta? Since z=beta*(c-cmaxmax), if zmax=0? Let's compute carefully.
```
Let `cbase = c.max()`? In previous: z=beta*(c - c_max); z_shifted=z-z.max() (z.max=0). L=c_max + (z.max - z_shifted.max + log(exp_sum))/beta. If no shift, exp_sum=∑exp(beta(c-cmax)); logsumexp = cmax + (1/β) log ∑exp(...). If we shift z by zmax (0), still L = cmax + (zmax + log(exp_sum))/β? Since exp_z=exp(z), z max 0, logsumexp original = max_c? Actually original LSE = (1/β) log ∑ exp(β c_k) = cmax + (1/β) log ∑ exp(β(c_k-cmax)). If we set z=β(c-cmax), zmax=0, L=cmax + log(exp_sum)/β. If we shift z by smax for stability, then exp_sum_shifted=∑exp(z-smax), log original = smax + log(exp_sum_shifted). But smax relative to cmax? If z=β(c-cmax), smax≤0. L = cmax + (smax + log(exp_sum_shifted))/β. Good.
So implement:
```
cmax=c.max()
z=beta*(c-cmax)
zmax=z.max()
ez=np.exp(z-zmax)
s=ez.sum()
L = cmax + (zmax + np.log(s))/beta
```
If beta large and zmax negative? zmax=0 if cmax included, but due no? okay.
`val = 2*n*L/(S*S)`.
Gradient: `grad_L = cross_weights_grad(x,p, factor=2)` (gradient of L w.r.t x) because p from LSE of c. Then `val_grad = 2*n*(grad_L/S² - 2L/S³)` (derivative of L*S^-2). Good. If p sum1.
Return val, grad.
```
def run_lbfgs(a0, beta, maxiter=2000, maxfun=10000):
   a0=clip...
   bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)]*n
   res=minimize(lse_objective, a0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, args=(beta,), options={...})
   a=clip(res.x); sc=score_sequence(a); return sc,a
```
Need beta raw. Choose beta = beta_scale / cmax_estimate. `cmax_est = score_sequence(a0)/(2*n)`. But `score_sequence` expensive; inside run we can compute once.
```

    cmax=conv_self(a0).max()
    if cmax<=0: return inf,a0
    beta=beta_scale/cmax
    ...
```
Use beta_scale values [20,100,500,2000]. Maxfun lower for early. Since objective nonconvex, L-BFGS may increase; compare best.
Be careful: In `lse_objective`, x might have very small sum if L-BFGS changes scale; gradient handles. If sum <0.01? Evaluator inf if sum<0.01, but objective scale invariant; if S tiny, huge. We can add barrier? D-BFGS bounds 0; starting sum1, gradient scale pushes to keep sum. Fine.

### LP alternating objective? Let's include but maybe less time.

Top-level `lp_fista`:
```
def lp_fista(a, M, n_iters=3000, beta_end=300, x_init=None):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1
   if x_init is None: x=np.ones(n)/n
   else ...
   u=x.copy(); t=1.0
   # ramp beta from 10 to beta_end over 20% iters
   for it in range(n_iters):
      frac=...
      beta=10 + (beta_end-10)*frac if ramp else beta_end
      c=conv_self? No conv a,x; use `conv_cross(a,x)` (linear convolution a and x)
      z=beta*(c-M)
      # softmax p length L
      zmax=z.max(); ez=exp(z-zmax); p=ez/sum
      bp=cross_weights_grad(a,p, factor=1.0) # no 2
      grad=bp - 1.0
      step=1.0/(beta + 1e-8)
      v=u - step*grad
      x_new=max(v,0)
      t_new=(1+sqrt(1+4t^2))/2
      u=x_new + (t-1)/t_new*(x_new-x)
      u=max(u,0)
      x=x_new; t=t_new
   return x
```

### Verify mathematical formula for score with factor 2

`score_sequence` uses 2*n*max/S². For uniform n=7000, score=2. Good target 1.502. Active gradient with F=cmax, step scale F/norm2. If objective score=2nF with S1, gradient score=2n g. The Lipschitz step for score would be score/norm_score² = (2nF)/(4n²norm2)=F/(2n norm2). But we use gradient of F and accept by score (constant factor), step same as for F? If objective is c*F (c>0), gradient c*g, norm² c²norm2; steepest step = (cF)/(c²norm2)=F/(c norm2). Wait derivative of objective O=cF: grad=cg. The quadratic step minimizing O+grad·d+... with norm regularization is d= - (F/norm2) g (c cancels). So using F/norm2 is correct. Good.

```
def random_smooth_sequence(n, rng, sigma=0.5):
   x=rng.standard_normal(n)
   # simple moving average with kernel length max(3,n//100)
   kernel=np.ones(max(3,n//100)); kernel/=kernel.sum()
   x=np.convolve(x,kernel,mode='same')
   a=0.5+sigma*np.tanh(x)  # nonnegative? tanh bounded [-1,1], 0.5±0.5 in [0,1]
   a=np.clip(a,1e-6,None)
   return a/a.sum()
```

### Let's code helper functions top-level robustly

#### Function: next_power_of_two
```
def next_power_of_two(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

#### convolve2
```
def convolve2(a,b):
    L=len(a)+len(b)-1
    Lf=next_power_of_two(L)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, Lf)
    B=np.fft.rfft(b, Lf)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*B, Lf)[:L]
```
For self use same maybe.

#### conv_self
Can call convolve2(a,a), but separate for speed (compute FFT once):
```
def conv_self(a):
  M=2*len(a)-1; Lf=next_power_of_two(M); A=np.fft.rfft(a,Lf); return np.fft.irfft(A*A,Lf)[:M]
```

#### score from convolution
```
def score_from_conv(a,c):
  n=len(a); S=float(np.sum(a)); if S<...; m=float(np.max(c)); return 2.0*n*m/(S*S)
```

#### score_sequence
```
def score_sequence(a):
  a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
  c=conv_self(a)
  return score_from_conv(a,c)
```
But for repeated, to avoid asarray overhead, fine.

#### project_simplex
Handle target 1. Ensure input 1D.
```
def project_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n=v.size
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)-1.0
    cond=u - cssv/np.arange(1,n+1) > 0
    if not np.any(cond):
       return np.ones(n)/n
    rho=np.arange(1,n+1)[cond].max()
    theta=cssv[cond].max()/rho
    out=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s>0: out=out/s
    return out
```
Rounding.

```
def random_smooth_sequence(n, rng, amp=0.5, kernel_frac=1/80):
   x=rng.standard_normal(n)
   k=max(3, int(n*kernel_frac))
   kernel=np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64); kernel/=kernel.sum()
   # For memory, use cumsum trick for 'same'
   x_pad=np.pad(x, (k//2,k//2), mode='edge')
   c=np.cumsum(x_pad)
   out=c[k:]-c[:-k]
   out/=(k*x_pad.mean()+1e-300)? Actually cumulative sum normalized: convolution sum = out_eff; kernel sum1, effective = out_eff. Since kernel defined sum1, out_eff is correct? For 'same', out_eff = c[k:]-c[:-k]. Good.
   a=1.0+amp*np.tanh(out_eff)
   a=np.clip(a,1e-6,None)
   S=a.sum(); if S<... a=ones
   return a/S
```
`out_eff` maybe same length. Good. But if k even, padding k//2 vs? Conv 'same' alignment not critical.

#### make_poly_seq and poly_objective
```
def make_poly_seq(params, basis):
    n=basis.shape[1]
    if params.size==0:
       a=np.ones(n)
    else:
       g=basis.dot(params)
       # estimate min
       minf=1.0+float(np.min(g))
       if minf < 1e-9:
          # scale down g to ensure 1+g >= 1e-8
          max_neg = max(1.0, -minf/0.999)
          g = g / max_neg
       a=1.0+g
       # If g produced values below due numerical
       a=np.clip(a, 1e-12, None)
       S=a.sum()
       if S <= 1e-300: return np.inf, np.ones(n)/n
       a=a/S
       return score_sequence(a), a
```
Scaling formula: If minf < 1e-9 (i.e., some 1+g negative), need scale g by `0.999/(-minf)`? Suppose minf=-0.5 (1+g min=0.5? Wait minf =1+min g. If minf<0, -minf = - (1+ming)=-(1+gmin). We want after scale g' = s g; new min =1 + s gmin =1 + s*(minf-1) =1 - s*(1-minf). Want =1e-6. s <= (1-1e-6)/(1-minf). Use `scale = 0.999/(1-minf)` if minf<0.999? If minf negative, denominator >1. Good. Simpler:
```
if minf < 1e-8:
    scale = (1.0 - 1e-8) / max(1.0, (1.0 - minf))
    g *= scale
```
If minf = -0.5, 1-minf=1.5, scale=0.666; new min=1+0.666*(-1.5)=0.001. Good. If minf =0.5 but we want margin? no scale.
But if g max huge, 1+g positive; no scale; score maybe high but feasible. Could cap amplitude to avoid numerical: if g max > 10, scale down to 10. Since normalization, large spikes maybe bad. Add:
```
maxg=float(np.max(g))
if maxg > 10.0: g *= 10.0/maxg
```
But this changes shape; okay for param search.
`poly_objective(params,basis): sc,_=make_poly_seq; return sc`

#### parameter_search
Top-level:
```
def parameter_search(basis, rng, initial_params_list, max_opts=8, max_iter=250, time_limit=60.0):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    best_sc=np.inf; best_a=None
    n=basis.shape[1]
    t0check time? In function, t_start=time.monotonic(); limit=t_start+time_limit
    for x0 in initial_params_list:
       if time.monotonic() > limit - 5: break
       sc,a=make_poly_seq(x0,basis)
       if sc<best_sc: update
       # Nelder-Mead maybe only if points left
       if time.monotonic() > limit - 10: continue
       try:
          res=minimize(poly_objective, x0, args=(basis,), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':max_iter,'xatol':1e-8,'fatol':1e-12,'adaptive':True})
          sc,a=make_poly_seq(res.x,basis)
          if sc<best_sc: update
       except Exception:
          pass
    return best_sc,best_a
```
Need `time` in function; okay. It uses global time. No closures.
Initial params generated in propose to pass.

#### Builds
```
def build_basis_odd(n,K):
   x=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)/(n-1)
   basis=np.zeros((K,n))
   for k in range(K): basis[k]=np.cos((2*k+1)*np.pi*x)
   return basis

def build_basis_cos_sin(n, freqs):
   x=...; m=2*len(freqs); basis=np.zeros((m,n))
   for idx,mfreq in enumerate(freqs):
      angle=2*np.pi*mfreq*x
      basis[2*idx]=np.cos(angle)
      basis[2*idx+1]=np.sin(angle)
   return basis
```

       std=[0.5,0.25,0.15,0.1,0.05,0.03][:K]

#### LP functions

`lp_fista(a, M, n_iters, beta_end, x_init)`. Need time? We can pass `time_limit` and check each iter.
```
def lp_fista(a, M, n_iters, beta_start=10.0, beta_end=300.0, x_init=None, time_limit=None):
   n=len(a); a=normalize_nonneg(a); L=2n-1
   if x_init is None: x=np.ones(n)/n
   else: x=np.maximum(np.asarray(x_init,float),0); if sum>0 x/=sum else ones/n
   u=x.copy(); t=1.0
   t0=time.monotonic()
   ramp_iters=max(1,int(0.2*n_iters))
   for it in range(n_iters):
      if time_limit is not None and time.monotonic() > time_limit: break
      if it < ramp_iters: beta=beta_start+(beta_end-beta_start)*it/ramp_iters
      else: beta=beta_end
      c=convolve2(a,x)
      z=beta*(c-M)
      zmax=float(np.max(z)); ez=np.exp(z-zmax); s=ez.sum()
      if not finite(s) or s<=0: p=np.ones(L)/L
      else: p=ez/s
      bp=cross_weights_grad(a,p,1.0)
      grad=bp - 1.0
      step=1.0/(beta+1e-8)
      v=u - step*grad
      x_new=np.maximum(v,0.0)
      # prevent blowup
      maxv=float(x_new.max())
      if not finite(maxv) or maxv>1e6:
          x_new=np.clip(x_new,0,1e3)
      t_new=0.5*(1+np.sqrt(1+4*t*t))
      u=x_new + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x_new-x)
      u=np.maximum(u,0.0)
      x=x_new; t=t_new
   return x
```
Use beta_start 5, beta_end 500 maybe. Step 1/β. Since B norm≤1, good. But if a sum normalized exactly 1; row sums ≤1, col sums=1. Good.
`lp_alternating` with time.

def lp_alternating(a0, budget_s=300, max_outer=6, fista_iters=3000, beta_end=300, time_limit_abs=None):

      x=lp_fista(a,M,iters,beta_end=beta_end,x_init=x_init,time_limit=limit-10)
      sx=x.sum(); if sx<... break
      x=x/sx

      for t in [0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.85]:

         at=clip; S=at.sum(); if S< continue; at=at/S
         sc=score_sequence(at); if sc<best_sc: ...
      if best_sc < best_f: update no=0 else no++
      a=best_at; x_init=x
      if no>=3: break
   return best_a,best_f

def polish_candidate(a0, budget_s, seed=1, desired_score=1.5027):

while ...:
  c=conv_self(a); F=float(c.max()); f_score=2*n*F
  if f_score < best_f? (should) update best? yes
  w = topk_weights(c, top_frac) if use_top else lse_weights(c,beta_scales[beta_idx])

#### run_lbfgs signature
```
def run_lbfgs(a0, beta_scale, maxiter=2000, maxfun=10000):
   a0=normalize_nonneg(...)
   cmax=conv_self(a0).max()
   beta=max(1.0, beta_scale/max(cmax,1e-300))
   bounds=[(0.0, 1000.0)]*n
   res=minimize(lse_objective, a0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, args=(beta,), options={...})
   a=clip; if all zero return
   sc=score_sequence(a)
   return sc,a
```
`lse_objective` top-level:
```
def lse_objective(x, beta):
   n=len(x)
   c=conv_self(x)
   S=float(np.sum(x))
   if S<1e-200: return 1e12, np.zeros(n)
   z=beta*(c-c.max())
   zmax=float(np.max(z))
   ez=np.exp(z-zmax)
   s=float(np.sum(ez))
   if s<=0 or not finite: p=zeros; L=c.max()
   else: p=ez/s; L=c.max() + (zmax+np.log(s))/beta
   val=2.0*n*L/(S*S)
   if not finite(val): return 1e12, zeros
   gradL=cross_weights_grad(x,p,2.0)
   grad=2.0*n*(gradL/(S*S) - 2.0*L/(S*S*S))
   return val, grad
```
If x has zeros and beta high, p okay. If beta huge and LSE approximates max, gradient fine. But `zmax` should be 0 if c.max included, but due c max repeated, zmax=0. Good.

def optimize_spectrum(a0,budget_s=300, desired_score=0.0):
  a=normalize_nonneg(...); best...; c=conv_self(a); t0=...
  n=len(a)
  for ratio in [0.985,0.97,0.95,0.92,0.88,0.83]:
    if time>limit: break
    for inner in range(10):
      if time>limit: break
      cmax=float(c.max()); cmean=float(c.mean())
      ratio_target=cmax*ratio
      if desired_score>0:
         abs_target=desired_score/(2.0*n)
         if abs_target < ratio_target: ratio_target=abs_target
      target=max(ratio_target, cmean*1.02, cmax*0.5)
      if cmax <= target+1e-15: break
      improved=False
      for lam in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.025,0.5):
         a_try=spectrum_update(a,c,target,lam)
         c_try=conv_self(a_try)
         f_try=2.0*n*float(c_try.max())/(np.sum(a_try)**2) # sum=1 but
         if f_try<best_f:
            a=a_try; best...; c=c_try; improved=True; break
      if not improved: break
  return best
```

def parameter_search(basis, starts, max_opts=3, max_iter=220, time_limit=60.0):
    t_start=time.monotonic(); limit=t_start+max(10.0,time_limit)
    best_sc=np.inf; best_a=None; n=basis.shape[1]
    opts_done=0
    for x0 in starts:
        if opts_done >= max_opts: break
        x0=np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        sc,a=make_poly_seq(x0,basis)
        if sc<best_sc: ...
        opts_done +=1
        if time.monotonic() > limit - 5: break
        try:
           res=minimize(poly_objective, x0, args=(basis,), method='Nelder-Mead', options=...)
           sc2,a2=make_poly_seq(res.x,basis)
           if sc2<best_sc: update
        except Exception:
           pass
        if time.monotonic() > limit - 10: break
    return best_sc,best_a
```

### Let's test mathematically with uniform sequence in active gradient:
- `project_simplex` of uniform - eta*grad maybe. For central peak uniform, grad=2/n all entries? ```python
"""
Non-negative step-height optimizer for minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

Strategy:
1. Build strong low-dimensional trigonometric candidates (odd anti-palindromic
   cosines and general low-frequency cosine/sine families), optimize their
   parameters with Nelder-Mead.
2. Use the best parent/parametric candidates as starting points for full
   high-dimensional polishing:
     - truncated spectrum shaping in the FFEZ domain,
     - projected gradient / LSE active-set search,
     - corrected LP alternating FISTA solver,
     - L-BFGS-B on a log-sum-exp smoothed objective.
All helpers are top-level and no closures/ lambdas are used.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def conv_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    L = next_power_of_two(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:M]
    return c


def convolve2(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    L = len(a) + len(b) - 1
    if L <= 1:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    Lf = next_power_of_two(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, Lf)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, Lf)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * B, Lf)[:L]


def score_from_conv(a, c):
    n = len(a)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return 2.0 * float(n) * m / (S * S)


def score_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    c = conv_self(a)
    return score_from_conv(a, c)


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / S


def project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    inds = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cssv = np.cumsum(u) - 1.0
    cond = u - cssv / inds > 0.0
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    rho = inds[cond].max()
    theta = cssv[cond].max() / rho
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(out))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return out / s


def cross_weights_grad(a, w, factor=2.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    L = len(w)
    M = n + L - 1
    if M <= 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    Lf = next_power_of_two(M)
    ar = a[::-1]
    AR = np.fft.rfft(ar, Lf)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, Lf)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(AR * W, Lf)[:M]
    return factor * conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def lse_weights(c, beta_scale):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if cmax <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, c.size)
    beta = float(beta_scale) / cmax
    z = beta * (c - cmax)
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(np.sum(ez))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, c.size)
    return ez / s


def topk_weights(c, frac):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = len(c)
    K = max(1, int(frac * L))
    if K >= L:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    idx = np.argpartition(c, L - K)[-K:]
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)
    return w


def build_basis_odd(n, K):
    if n <= 1:
        x = np.zeros(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    basis = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(K):
        basis[k] = np.cos((2 * k + 1) * np.pi * x)
    return basis


def build_basis_cos_sin(n, freqs):
    if n <= 1:
        x = np.zeros(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    m = 2 * len(freqs)
    basis = np.zeros((m, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for idx, f in enumerate(freqs):
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * x
        basis[2 * idx] = np.cos(ang)
        basis[2 * idx + 1] = np.sin(ang)
    return basis


def random_smooth_sequence(n, rng, amp=0.55, kernel_frac=1.0 / 80.0):
    x = rng.standard_normal(n)
    k = max(3, int(n * kernel_frac))
    kernel = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / k
    pad = k // 2
    x_pad = np.pad(x, (pad, pad), mode="edge")
    c = np.cumsum(x_pad)
    out = c[k:] - c[:-k]
    out = out[:n]
    a = 1.0 + amp * np.tanh(out)
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-9, None)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return a / S


def make_poly_seq(params, basis):
    n = basis.shape[1]
    if params.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        a = a / n
        return score_sequence(a), a

    params = np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(params)):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        return np.inf, a

    try:
        g = basis.dot(params)
    except Exception:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        return np.inf, a

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        return np.inf, a

    gmin = float(np.min(g))
    if 1.0 + gmin < 1e-9:
        scale = (1.0 - 1e-9) / max(1.0, 1.0 - gmin)
        g = g * scale

    gmax = float(np.max(g))
    if gmax > 50.0:
        g = g * (50.0 / max(1.0, gmax))

    a = 1.0 + g
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-12, None)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        return np.inf, a
    a = a / S
    return score_sequence(a), a


def poly_objective(params, basis):
    try:
        sc, _ = make_poly_seq(params, basis)
        return float(sc)
    except Exception:
        return 1e15


def parameter_search(basis, starts, max_opts=3, max_iter=260, time_limit=60.0):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    limit = t_start + max(10.0, float(time_limit))
    best_sc = np.inf
    best_a = None
    n = basis.shape[1]

    opts_done = 0
    for x0 in starts:
        if opts_done >= max_opts:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > limit - 5.0:
            break

        x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if x0.size != basis.shape[0]:
            x0 = x0[:basis.shape[0]] if x0.size > basis.shape[0] else np.pad(x0, (0, basis.shape[0] - x0.size))

        sc, a = make_poly_seq(x0, basis)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        opts_done += 1
        if time.monotonic() > limit - 8.0:
            break

        try:
            res = minimize(
                poly_objective,
                x0,
                method="Nelder-Mead",
                args=(basis,),
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(max_iter),
                    "xatol": 1e-8,
                    "fatol": 1e-12,
                    "adaptive": True,
                },
            )
            sc2, a2 = make_poly_seq(res.x, basis)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc2
                best_a = a2.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

        if time.monotonic() > limit - 10.0:
            break

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        best_sc = score_sequence(best_a)
    return float(best_sc), best_a


def make_odd_starts(K, rng):
    starts = []
    std = [0.55, 0.25, 0.15, 0.10, 0.06, 0.04, 0.02]

    b = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    b[0] = 0.65
    starts.append(b.copy())

    b = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    b[0] = 0.72
    starts.append(b.copy())

    b = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    b[0] = 0.72
    if K > 1:
        b[1] = -0.08
    if K > 2:
        b[2] = 0.05
    starts.append(b.copy())

    b = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    b[0] = 0.78
    if K > 1:
        b[1] = -0.14
    if K > 2:
        b[2] = -0.04
    starts.append(b.copy())

    b = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    b[0] = -0.65
    starts.append(b.copy())

    for _ in range(3):
        s = np.array(std[:K], dtype=np.float64)
        p = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=K) * s
        starts.append(p)

    return starts


def make_general_starts(K, rng):
    starts = []
    std = [0.50, 0.20, 0.15, 0.10, 0.08, 0.05, 0.04, 0.03, 0.02]

    b = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    b[0] = 0.65
    starts.append(b.copy())

    if K >= 6:
        b = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
        b[0] = 0.55
        b[2] = -0.12
        starts.append(b.copy())

    if K >= 6:
        b = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
        b[0] = 0.60
        b[1] = 0.12
        starts.append(b.copy())

    if K >= 4:
        b = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
        b[0] = -0.60
        b[2] = 0.10
        starts.append(b.copy())

    for _ in range(3):
        s = np.array(std[:K], dtype=np.float64)
        p = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=K) * s
        starts.append(p)

    return starts


def spectrum_update(a, c, target, lam):
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    Lf = next_power_of_two(L)

    U = np.fft.rfft(a, Lf)
    excess = np.maximum(c - target, 0.0)
    if float(np.max(excess)) <= 1e-300:
        return a

    delta = -excess.copy()
    delta += float(np.sum(excess)) / float(L)

    dpad = np.zeros(Lf, dtype=np.float64)
    dpad[:L] = delta
    df = np.fft.rfft(dpad)

    denom = 2.0 * U
    dU = np.zeros_like(U, dtype=np.complex128)
    scale = max(1.0, float(np.max(np.abs(U))))
    ok = np.abs(denom) > (1e-14 * scale)
    dU[ok] = df[ok] / denom[ok]

    U_new = U + float(lam) * dU
    U_new[0] = float(np.sum(a))

    a_new = np.fft.irfft(U_new, Lf)[:n]
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a_new))
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    return a_new / S


def optimize_spectrum(a0, budget_s=300.0, desired_score=TARGET_SCORE):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_f = score_sequence(a)
    c = conv_self(a)
    n = len(a)

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    limit = t_start + max(10.0, float(budget_s))

    ratios = [0.9990, 0.9970, 0.9950, 0.9900, 0.9850, 0.9750, 0.9600, 0.9400, 0.9150, 0.8850]
    for ratio in ratios:
        if time.monotonic() > limit - 2.0:
            break
        for _ in range(8):
            if time.monotonic() > limit - 2.0:
                break

            cmax = float(np.max(c))
            cmean = float(np.mean(c))
            target = cmax * float(ratio)

            if desired_score > 0.0:
                abs_target = desired_score / (2.0 * float(n))
                if abs_target < target:
                    target = abs_target

            target = max(target, cmean * 1.005, cmax * 0.50)
            if cmax <= target + 1e-15:
                break

            improved = False
            for lam in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.025, 0.50):
                if time.monotonic() > limit - 2.0:
                    break
                a_try = spectrum_update(a, c, target, lam)
                c_try = conv_self(a_try)
                f_try = score_from_conv(a_try, c_try)
                if np.isfinite(f_try) and f_try < best_f - 1e-12:
                    a = a_try
                    c = c_try
                    best_f = f_try
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    improved = True
                    break
            if not improved:
                break

    return best_a, float(best_f)


def optimize_active_gradient(
    a0,
    budget_s=600.0,
    beta_scales=(20.0, 100.0, 500.0, 2000.0),
    top_frac=0.02,
    seed=1,
):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    n = len(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_f = score_sequence(a)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    limit = t_start + max(10.0, float(budget_s))

    it = 0
    beta_idx = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < limit - 2.0:
        c = conv_self(a)
        F = float(np.max(c))
        cur_f = 2.0 * float(n) * F
        if np.isfinite(cur_f) and cur_f < best_f - 1e-12:
            best_f = cur_f
            best_a = a.copy()

        if (it % 6) == 0:
            w = topk_weights(c, top_frac)
        else:
            w = lse_weights(c, float(beta_scales[min(beta_idx, len(beta_scales) - 1)]))

        g = cross_weights_grad(a, w, factor=2.0)
        g = g - (float(np.sum(g)) / float(n))
        norm2 = float(np.dot(g, g))

        if norm2 <= 1e-300:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 30 and beta_idx < len(beta_scales) - 1:
                beta_idx += 1
            it += 1
            continue

        scale = F / max(norm2, 1e-300)
        accepted = False

        for mult in (0.20, 0.10, 0.05, 0.02, 0.005, 0.001):
            eta = min(1.0, float(mult) * max(0.0, scale))
            if eta <= 0.0:
                continue
            a_try = project_simplex(a - eta * g)
            c_try = conv_self(a_try)
            f_try = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c_try)) / (float(np.sum(a_try)) ** 2)
            if np.isfinite(f_try) and f_try < best_f - 1e-12:
                a = a_try
                c = c_try
                best_f = f_try
                best_a = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 70:
                if beta_idx < len(beta_scales) - 1 and (no_improve % 35 == 0):
                    beta_idx += 1
                else:
                    base = best_a if (no_improve % 2 == 0) else a
                    noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
                    a = base * (1.0 + 0.025 * noise)
                    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
                    S = float(np.sum(a))
                    if S > 1e-300:
                        a = a / S
                    else:
                        a = best_a.copy()
                    no_improve = 0

        it += 1

    return best_a, float(best_f)


def lse_objective(x, beta):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(x)
    S = float(np.sum(x))
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = conv_self(x)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    z = beta * (c - cmax)
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(np.sum(ez))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, c.size)
        L = cmax
    else:
        p = ez / s
        L = cmax + (zmax + np.log(s)) / beta

    val = 2.0 * float(n) * float(L) / (S * S)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    gradL = cross_weights_grad(x, p, factor=2.0)
    S2 = S * S
    S3 = S2 * S
    grad = 2.0 * float(n) * (gradL / S2 - 2.0 * float(L) / S3)
    return val, grad


def run_lbfgs(a0, beta_scale, maxiter=1500, maxfun=5000):
    a0 = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    n = len(a0)
    cmax = float(np.max(conv_self(a0)))
    if cmax <= 1e-300:
        return score_sequence(a0), a0

    beta = max(1.0, float(beta_scale) / max(1e-300, cmax))
    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * n

    sc_in = score_sequence(a0)
    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_objective,
            a0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            args=(beta,),
            options={
                "ftol": 1e-15,
                "gtol": 1e-12,
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                "maxls": 30,
            },
        )
        a_out = np.nan_to_num(res.x, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        a_out = np.clip(a_out, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if float(np.sum(a_out)) <= 1e-12:
            return sc_in, a0
        sc_out = score_sequence(a_out)
        if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < sc_in:
            return float(sc_out), a_out
    except Exception:
        pass

    return float(sc_in), a0


def lp_fista(a, M, n_iters=3000, beta_start=5.0, beta_end=300.0, x_init=None, time_limit=None):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    if x_init is None:
        x = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    else:
        x = np.asarray(x_init, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
        sx = float(np.sum(x))
        if sx <= 1e-300:
            x = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        else:
            x = x / sx

    u = x.copy()
    t = 1.0
    ramp_iters = max(1, int(0.2 * n_iters))
    t_start = time.monotonic()

    for it in range(n_iters):
        if time_limit is not None and time.monotonic() > float(time_limit):
            break

        frac = float(it) / float(ramp_iters) if it < ramp_iters else 1.0
        beta = float(beta_start) + (float(beta_end) - float(beta_start)) * frac

        c = convolve2(a, x)
        z = beta * (c - float(M))
        zmax = float(np.max(z))
        ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
        s = float(np.sum(ez))
        if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
            p = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        else:
            p = ez / s

        bp = cross_weights_grad(a, p, factor=1.0)
        grad = bp - 1.0
        step = 1.0 / (beta + 1e-8)

        v = u - step * grad
        x_new = np.maximum(v, 0.0)

        mx = float(np.max(x_new)) if x_new.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx > 1e6:
            x_new = np.nan_to_num(x_new, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            x_new = np.clip(x_new, 0.0, 1000.0)

        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
        u = x_new + ((t - 1.0) / t_new) * (x_new - x)
        u = np.maximum(u, 0.0)
        x = x_new
        t = t_new

    return x


def lp_alternating(a0, budget_s=300.0, max_outer=5, fista_iters=3000, time_limit_abs=None):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_f = score_sequence(a)
    x_init = None
    no_improve = 0

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    if time_limit_abs is None:
        limit = t_start + max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    else:
        limit = float(time_limit_abs)

    for _ in range(max_outer):
        if time.monotonic() > limit - 15.0:
            break
        rem = max(0.0, limit - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 60.0:
            break

        c = conv_self(a)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M <= 1e-300:
            break

        iters = int(max(700, min(fista_iters, rem / 0.12)))
        x = lp_fista(
            a,
            M,
            n_iters=iters,
            beta_start=5.0,
            beta_end=350.0,
            x_init=x_init,
            time_limit=limit - 25.0,
        )
        sx = float(np.sum(x))
        if sx <= 1e-300:
            break
        x = x / sx
        x_init = x.copy()

        a_now = a.copy()
        f_now = best_f

        for t in (0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.45, 0.60, 0.75, 0.88):
            at = (1.0 - t) * a_now + t * x
            at = np.maximum(at, 0.0)
            S = float(np.sum(at))
            if S <= 1e-300:
                continue
            at = at / S
            sc = score_sequence(at)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < f_now:
                f_now = sc
                a_now = at

        if np.isfinite(f_now) and f_now < best_f - 1e-12:
            best_f = f_now
            best_a = a_now.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            a = a_now.copy()
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve >= 3:
            break

    return best_a, float(best_f)


def polish_candidate(a0, budget_s=300.0, seed=1, desired_score=TARGET_SCORE):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_f = score_sequence(a)

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    limit = t_start + max(20.0, float(budget_s))
    n = len(a)

    rem = limit - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 90.0 and best_f > desired_score - 1e-6:
        a_try, f_try = optimize_spectrum(a, min(180.0, rem * 0.25), desired_score)
        if np.isfinite(f_try) and f_try < best_f - 1e-12:
            best_a = a_try.copy()
            best_f = f_try
            a = a_try.copy()

    rem = limit - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80.0 and best_f > desired_score - 1e-6:
        a_try, f_try = optimize_active_gradient(
            a,
            min(0.45 * float(budget_s), rem - 20.0),
            beta_scales=(20.0, 100.0, 500.0, 2000.0),
            top_frac=0.02,
            seed=seed,
        )
        if np.isfinite(f_try) and f_try < best_f - 1e-12:
            best_a = a_try.copy()
            best_f = f_try
            a = a_try.copy()

    rem = limit - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 220.0 and n <= 14000 and best_f > desired_score - 1e-6:
        a_try, f_try = lp_alternating(
            a,
            min(0.18 * float(budget_s), rem - 60.0),
            max_outer=4,
            fista_iters=3500,
            time_limit_abs=limit - 70.0,
        )
        if np.isfinite(f_try) and f_try < best_f - 1e-12:
            best_a = a_try.copy()
            best_f = f_try
            a = a_try.copy()

    rem = limit - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120.0 and n <= 16000 and best_f > desired_score - 1e-6:
        a_try, f_try = run_lbfgs(a, 120.0, maxiter=1200, maxfun=3500)
        if np.isfinite(f_try) and f_try < best_f - 1e-12:
            best_a = a_try.copy()
            best_f = f_try
            a = a_try.copy()

    rem = limit - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80.0 and best_f > desired_score - 1e-6:
        a_try, f_try = run_lbfgs(a, 600.0, maxiter=800, maxfun=2500)
        if np.isfinite(f_try) and f_try < best_f - 1e-12:
            best_a = a_try.copy()
            best_f = f_try
            a = a_try.copy()

    rem = limit - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 70.0 and best_f > desired_score - 1e-6:
        a_try, f_try = optimize_active_gradient(
            a,
            rem - 10.0,
            beta_scales=(500.0, 2000.0, 8000.0),
            top_frac=0.005,
            seed=seed + 17,
        )
        if np.isfinite(f_try) and f_try < best_f - 1e-12:
            best_a = a_try.copy()
            best_f = f_try

    return best_a, float(best_f)


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 2400.0
    budget = max(120.0, budget)
    deadline = t0 + max(90.0, budget - 60.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(20260420)

    fallback = np.full(1024, 1.0 / 1024.0, dtype=np.float64)
    best_score = score_sequence(fallback)
    best_a = fallback.copy()

    parent = None
    try:
        p = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if p is not None:
            p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size >= 64:
                if p.size > 18000:
                    m = 12288
                    p = np.interp(
                        np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m),
                        np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size),
                        p,
                    )
                parent = normalize_nonneg(p)
                ps = score_sequence(parent)
                print(f"parent length={len(parent)} score={ps:.8f}", flush=True)
                if np.isfinite(ps) and ps < best_score:
                    best_score = ps
                    best_a = parent.copy()
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    lengths_to_search = set()
    if parent is not None and len(parent) <= 18000:
        lengths_to_search.add(len(parent))
    for n_try in (12288, 8192, 6962, 4096):
        if n_try not in lengths_to_search:
            lengths_to_search.add(n_try)
    lengths_to_search = sorted(lengths_to_search)[:4]

    length_best = {}
    if best_a is not None:
        length_best[len(best_a)] = (best_score, best_a.copy())
    if parent is not None:
        ps = score_sequence(parent)
        Lp = len(parent)
        if Lp not in length_best or ps < length_best[Lp][0]:
            length_best[Lp] = (ps, parent.copy())

    phase1_deadline = t0 + 0.12 * budget
    phase1_deadline = max(t0 + 60.0, min(phase1_deadline, deadline - 180.0))

    print("Phase 1: parametric search", flush=True)

    for n in lengths_to_search:
        if time.monotonic() > phase1_deadline - 20.0:
            break

        if n < 256 or n > 16384:
            continue

        rem = phase1_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 40.0:
            break

        K_odd = 6 if n <= 8192 else 5
        basis_odd = build_basis_odd(n, K_odd)
        starts_odd = make_odd_starts(K_odd, rng)
        tl = min(70.0, max(25.0, rem * 0.55))
        sc_o, a_o = parameter_search(basis_odd, starts_odd, max_opts=3, max_iter=260, time_limit=tl)
        if np.isfinite(sc_o) and a_o is not None:
            if sc_o < best_score:
                best_score = sc_o
                best_a = a_o.copy()
                print(f"param odd n={n} new best={sc_o:.8f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s", flush=True)
            if n not in length_best or sc_o < length_best[n][0]:
                length_best[n] = (sc_o, a_o.copy())

        if time.monotonic() > phase1_deadline - 25.0:
            continue

        rem = phase1_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 35.0:
            break

        freqs = (1, 2, 3)
        basis_gen = build_basis_cos_sin(n, freqs)
        K_gen = basis_gen.shape[0]
        starts_gen = make_general_starts(K_gen, rng)
        tl = min(70.0, max(25.0, rem * 0.55))
        sc_g, a_g = parameter_search(basis_gen, starts_gen, max_opts=3, max_iter=240, time_limit=tl)
        if np.isfinite(sc_g) and a_g is not None:
            if sc_g < best_score:
                best_score = sc_g
                best_a = a_g.copy()
                print(f"param gen n={n} new best={sc_g:.8f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s", flush=True)
            if n not in length_best or sc_g < length_best[n][0]:
                length_best[n] = (sc_g, a_g.copy())

    if time.monotonic() < phase1_deadline - 10.0 and best_a is not None:
        for _ in range(3):
            if time.monotonic() > phase1_deadline - 8.0:
                break
            a_rand = random_smooth_sequence(len(best_a), rng)
            sc_rand = score_sequence(a_rand)
            if np.isfinite(sc_rand) and sc_rand < best_score:
                best_score = sc_rand
                best_a = a_rand.copy()
                print(f"random smooth new best={sc_rand:.8f}", flush=True)

    phase2_deadline = t0 + 0.85 * budget
    phase2_deadline = max(phase1_deadline + 60.0, min(phase2_deadline, deadline - 150.0))

    print("Phase 2: high-dimensional polishing", flush=True)

    cand_list = [(best_score, best_a.copy())]
    cand_lengths = [len(best_a)]

    if parent is not None:
        ps = score_sequence(parent)
        Lp = len(parent)
        if Lp not in cand_lengths and ps < best_score + 0.12:
            cand_list.append((ps, parent.copy()))
            cand_lengths.append(Lp)

    for n in lengths_to_search:
        if len(cand_list) >= 3:
            break
        if n in cand_lengths:
            continue
        if n in length_best:
            scn, an = length_best[n]
            if np.isfinite(scn) and scn < best_score + 0.08:
                cand_list.append((scn, an.copy()))
                cand_lengths.append(n)

    # Sort candidates manually by score without lambda.
    # There are at most three candidates.
    if len(cand_list) == 3:
        i0, i1, i2 = 0, 1, 2
        if cand_list[i1][0] < cand_list[i0][0]:
            i0, i1 = i1, i0
        if cand_list[i2][0] < cand_list[i0][0]:
            i0, i2 = i2, i0
        if cand_list[i1][0] < cand_list[i0][0]:
            i0, i1 = i1, i0
        if cand_list[i2][0] < cand_list[i1][0]:
            i1, i2 = i2, i1
        cand_list = [cand_list[i0], cand_list[i1], cand_list[i2]]
    elif len(cand_list) == 2:
        if cand_list[1][0] < cand_list[0][0]:
            cand_list = [cand_list[1], cand_list[0]]

    fracs = [0.58, 0.29, 0.13]
    rem_total = phase2_deadline - time.monotonic()
    used = 0.0
    for idx, (sc_c, a_c) in enumerate(cand_list):
        if time.monotonic() > phase2_deadline - 30.0:
            break
        if idx >= len(fracs):
            frac = 0.05
        else:
            frac = fracs[idx]
        budget_c = max(0.0, rem_total * frac)
        if idx < len(cand_list) - 1:
            used += budget_c
        else:
            budget_c = max(0.0, phase2_deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0)

        if budget_c < 60.0:
            break

        print(
            f"polish candidate {idx} n={len(a_c)} start={sc_c:.8f} budget={budget_c:.0f}s",
            flush=True,
        )
        a_new, sc_new = polish_candidate(a_c, budget_c, seed=100 + 13 * (idx + 1))
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
            best_score = sc_new
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            L = len(a_new)
            if L not in length_best or sc_new < length_best[L][0]:
                length_best[L] = (sc_new, a_new.copy())
            print(f"candidate {idx} improved to {sc_new:.8f}", flush=True)

    final_budget = max(0.0, deadline - 25.0 - time.monotonic())
    if final_budget > 80.0 and best_a is not None:
        print("Phase 3: final polish", flush=True)
        a_new, sc_new = polish_candidate(best_a, final_budget, seed=9999)
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
            best_score = sc_new
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            print(f"final polish improved to {sc_new:.8f}", flush=True)

    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64), nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(np.sum(out_arr))
    if S < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.full(1024, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)

    final_score = score_sequence(out_arr)
    print(f"FINAL internal score={final_score:.8f} length={len(out_arr)} time={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s", flush=True)

    return [float(x) for x in out_arr]
```
